We can maintain c_k array and update incrementally for coordinate changes. For continuous change of h_i by δ (or pair), c_k changes:
For k>=0, c_k = ∑_{p=0}^{n-k-1} h_p (1-h_{p+k}). If h_i changes by δ, for each k:
- if i in first range: c_k += δ(1 - h_{i+k}); also if i in second range (i>=k): old term with p=i-k: product h_{i-k}(1-h_i) changes by -δ h_{i-k}. So Δc_k = δ[(1 - h_{i+k}) if i+k<n else 0] - δ[h_{i-k} if i-k>=0 else 0]. For k=0, change is derivative 2? Actually product h_i(1-h_i) changes δ(1-2h_i). Formula with both? For k=0, first and second same pair? Use special.
For a pair change δ at i +δ, j -δ, Δc_k = δ coeff(i,k) - δ coeff(j,k), where coeff(i,k)= (1-h_{i+k})I(i+k<n) - h_{i-k}I(i-k>=0) for k>0, and k=0 coeff = 1-2h_i? But product for k=0 = h_i(1-h_i); if h_i changes δ, Δ=δ(1-2h_i) - δ². Linear coeff 1-2h_i. If both i and j affect k=0. We can maintain c vector by adding δ * (coef_i - coef_j). How to compute coefficient vector for a coordinate i efficiently?
Given h array length n, M=2n-1. For coordinate i, we need for all lags k = -(n-1)..n-1:
coef = (1 - h[i+k]) if 0≤i+k<n else 0 minus h[i-k] if 0≤i-k<n else 0.
This can be computed by slices:
coef = np.zeros(M)
m0 = n-1 - k? Actually index = k + (n-1).
For first: k ranges from -i to n-1-i. idx from n-1-i to n-1+n-1-i? Let's create k array np.arange(-(n-1), n) maybe but per coordinate loops? Better precompute for all coordinates? Coefficient matrix update needed. But for candidate pairs, we can compute d = coef_i - coef_j on fly. For many pairs, need coefficients for involved i. We can cache coef vectors for current h and invalidate when h changes? If update only few coordinates, most coefficients for unaffected coordinates? Let's examine coefficient for coordinate i depends on all entries h[i+k] for k not 0: uses values at i plus all other positions via k. When h at positions i and j change, coefficient for a coordinate p changes if p+k is i or j for some k => p = i±k or j±k, which can be any p (choose k=p-i). Thus every coordinate's coefficient changes! So caching full matrix invalid after any move. But we can compute coefficient for a single i in O(n) with vectorized:
coef = (1 - h_n_shift?) maybe:
For first part: an array A_i[k] = 1-h_{i+k} when in range. This is 1 over centered window of length? Use precomputed all-ones M and subtract h values placed at indices idx = k+n-1 where j=i+k. The partner positions j range 0..n-1, with k=j-i. So idx = j-i+n-1. So first part sets those idx to 1 - h[j], others 0.
Second part: B_i[k] = -h_{i-k} where i-k in range; partner j=i-k, k=i-j, idx=i-j+n-1. Set to -h[j].
Thus coefficient vector for i can be built by taking zeros, adding to slice idx1 = n-1 + (0 - i) .. n-1 + (n-1 - i) step 1: values 1-h[0:n]. For second: idx2 = n-1 + (i -0) .. n-1 + (i-(n-1)): values -h[0:n] (but reversed? idx = n-1 + i - j, as j increases idx decreases. So assign idx2 in descending order. We can do:
coef = zeros(M)
coef[n-1-i : n-1-i+n] += 1 - h  # j=0..n-1 -> k=j-i
coef[n-1+i : n-1+i-n+1? need slice descending for second]
For second j=0..n-1, idx = n-1 + i - j. This is from n-1+i down to n-1+i-(n-1)=i. Step -1. Assign -h (in normal order?) If slice coef[i : n :?] with step -1: coef[n-1+i : i-1 : -1] = -h? Let's test: j=0 idx=n-1+i; j=1 idx=n-1+i-1; ... j=n-1 idx=i. So coef[n-1+i : i-1 : -1] = -h[0:n]. Yes. For first: j=0 idx=n-1-i; ... j=n-1 idx=n-1-i+n-1? Actually idx = n-1 + (j-i) = n-1-i+j. Range from n-1-i to n-1-i+n-1 = 2n-2-i. Step +1. So coef[n-1-i : 2n-1-i] += 1-h.
Need ensure slice end exclusive: n-1-i+n = 2n-1-i. Good.

For active A length M≤100, for each i we can compute vector length M by directly indexing h:
For each active lag k (m), coef_i = (1-h[i+k]) if valid - h[i-k] if valid. Loop over A and vectorize over i:
coef_act = zeros(n, lenA)
for each a:
  k = m-(n-1)
  if k>=0:
    idx_first = 0:n-k; coef_act[idx_first,a] += 1 - h[k:n]
    idx_second = k:n; coef_act[idx_second,a] += -h[0:n-k]
  else l=-k:
    idx_first = l:n; += 1 - h[0:n-l]
    idx_second=0:n-l; += -h[l:n]
This loops M_act (≤100) with vector additions length n: 50k ops per iteration, fine. This gives coefficient for active lags for all i. Great.

Full coefficient function for coordinate i (np):
```
def coefficient_vector(i,h,n):
  M=2*n-1
  ccoef=np.zeros(M)
  ccoef[n-1+i : i-1 : -1] += 1.0 - h
  ccoef[n-1-i : 2*n-1-i] += -h
  return ccoef
```
Test slices boundaries: i=0: first slice n-1: -1 : -1 length n, indices n-1 down to 0. second n-1: n-1+n? n-1:2n-1? length n? start n-1, stop 2n-1 exclusive, indices n-1..2n-2. Good. i=n-1: first n-1+n-1=2n-2 down to n-1, length n. second n-1-(n-1)=0 to 2n-1-(n-1)=n, length n. Covers all. For n=2: okay.

Active coefficient matrix for all i:
```
def active_coef_matrix(h, n, idxs):
  M=len(idxs)
  ca=np.zeros((n,M))
  m0=n-1
  for a,m in enumerate(idxs):
    r=m-m0
    if r>=0:
      l1=r; l2=n-r
      ca[l1:l2, a] += 1.0 - h[:l2]
      ca[:l2, a] += -h[r:]
    else:
      l=-r
      l2=n-l
      ca[:l2, a] += 1.0 - h[l:]
      ca[l:, a] += -h[:l2]
  return ca
```
Need ensure when r=0: first ca[0:n]+=1-h, second ca[0:n]+=-h => 1-2h. The code r>=0 with l2=n: ca[0:n]+=1-h; ca[:n]+=-h. OK.

Candidate exact eval:
```
def pair_direction_delta(h, n, c, i, j):
  if i==j: return None
  ci=coefficient_vector(i,h,n); cj=coefficient_vector(j,h,n)
  d=ci-cj  # i inc, j dec linear
  M=2*n-1
  # q sparse
  q0 = M
  dabs=abs(i-j)
  # We'll evaluate at candidate delta values only, no need q full maybe add delta^2 at q indices.
```
For f at δ: c_trial = c + δ*d. Then adjust quadratic: c_trial[n-1] += δ² * (-2); if dabs>0: c_trial[n-1+dabs] += δ²; c_trial[n-1-dabs] += δ². If i,j? Both lags within 0..M. Yes.
But if dabs > n-1? max n-1, ok.

def generate_candidate_pairs(h,n,ca,idxs,c):
  pairs=[]
  A=len(idxs)
  scores_weighted=[]
  cmax=c.max()
  # weights: uniform, softmax beta=2, softmax beta=10
  for w:
     S = ca.dot(w)
     low = argpartition S, K; high = ...
     filter low h<1-eps, high h>eps
     add cross low highs
  # per lag K=2 each:
  for a in range(min(A,20)):
     col=ca[:,a]
     low = smallest col top3; high = largest top3
     add cross
  # random: use current low/high top 5
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2
  for it in range(100):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    if diff>0:
      mask=h<1-1e-14; room=1-h[mask]; if room.sum>0: h[mask]+=diff*room/room.sum
    else:
      mask=h>1e-14; tot=h[mask]; h[mask]+=diff*tot/tot.sum? Actually to reduce, scale down: h[mask] *= h.sum? Need distribute proportional to h? Simpler: h[mask] += diff * h[mask] / h[mask].sum() (negative), which reduces larger more. If underflow, set zeros.

```

Function:
```
def trial_max_with_delta(c, d, delta, n, i, j):
    ct = c + delta * d
    ct[n-1] += -2.0*delta*delta
    dabs = abs(i-j)
    if dabs > 0:
       ct[n-1 + dabs] += delta*delta
       ct[n-1 - dabs] += delta*delta
    return float(np.max(ct))
```

```
def make_deltas(c, d, plus, minus, cmax):
  dlist=[]
  if plus > 1e-12:
    pos = d > 1e-12
    if pos.any():
       tau = np.min((cmax - c[pos]) / d[pos])
       if tau < 0: tau=0.0
       maxstep=min(plus, tau)
    else: maxstep=plus
    if maxstep>1e-10:
       for f in (0.8,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.05,0.02):
          dlist.append(f*maxstep)
  if minus >1e-12:
    neg = d < -1e-12
    if neg.any():
       tau=np.min((cmax-c[neg])/(-d[neg]))
       if tau<0: tau=0.0
       maxstep=min(minus,tau)
    else: maxstep=minus
    if maxstep>1e-10:
       for f in (...):
          dlist.append(-f*maxstep)
  # add tiny fixed if plus/minus > ...
  # unique with tolerance
```

Random lowpass binary construction:
```
def random_lowpass_h(n, seed, window):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  z=rng.randn(n)
  # moving average
  k=max(2, n//window?)
  smooth=np.convolve(z, np.ones(k)/k, mode='same')
  # threshold balanced: choose indices top n/2 by smooth as 1
  vals=smooth
  if n even:
    h=np.zeros(n); order=np.argsort(-vals); h[order[:n//2]]=1
  return project_feasible(h,n)
```

def active_gradient_step(h,n,c,act,weights,rho):
  ca=active_coef_matrix(...)
  g=ca.T @ weights
  g -= g.mean()  # to preserve sum approximately
  # clip g to max norm rho
  g = np.clip(g, -rho, rho)
  # adjust sum exactly with water-filling on g? We can do line search θ such that h+θg stays bounds.
```

def randomized_perturb(h,n,seed,strength):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   h2=h.copy()
   # move small mass: choose random pairs
   m = max(1, n//20)
   for _ in range(m):
      i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
      step=strength
      if h2[i]<=1-step and h2[j]>=step: h2[i]+=step; h2[j]-=step
   return project_feasible(h2,n)
```

Implement `random_lowpass_binary(n, seed, window_frac, sine_ampl=0.0)`:
```
def random_lowpass_binary(n, seed, window_frac, use_sine=True):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  z=rng.randn(n)
  k=max(3, int(n*window_frac))
  if k%2==0: k+=1
  smooth=np.convolve(z, np.ones(k)/k, mode='same')
  if use_sine:
     x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
     f=rng.choice([1,2,3,4])
     ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
     amp=rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)*smooth.std()
     smooth = smooth + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
  h=np.zeros(n)
  # balanced threshold: top n//2 1, if n odd maybe target n/2 float? Use n/2 rounded? Need sum target n/2 (float). For binary, n even preferred. Use int(round(n/2)).
  m=int(round(n/2))
  order=np.argsort(-smooth)
  h[order[:m]]=1.0
  return project_feasible(h,n)
```
If n odd, m= n//2? target n/2=integer+.5, binary sum can't exact; project will make fractional. We can just use n even for coarse. If parent n odd? use base even 500? But objective for return can be odd? dx=2/n. Target sum n/2 float. Coarse use even.

`downsample_upsample(h, m)`:
```
def interpolate_h(h, n_new):
   n_old=len(h)
   if n_new==n_old: return h.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
   return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
```

def generate_candidate_pairs(h,n,ca,idx,c,cmax,cap):
  pairs=[]; seen=set()
  A=len(idx)
  if A<=0: return pairs
  K=12; Kper=3
  # filter arrays
  can_inc = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
  can_dec = h > 1.0 + 1e-12
  # weighted scores
  # use top A maybe all
  topc = c[idx]
  weights_list=[]
  wu = np.ones(A); wu[:min(A,40)]? Actually uniform all active or top 40? Use uniform over all A.
  weights_list.append(wu/wu.sum())
  # softmax beta=5
  x=topc - topc.max(); w=np.exp(5*x); w/=w.sum()
  weights_list.append(w)
  if A>10:
     # top 20 softmax beta=30
     order=np.argsort(-topc)[:20]
     w2=np.zeros(A); vals=topc[order]-topc[order].max(); w2[order]=np.exp(30*vals); w2/=w2.sum(); weights_list.append(w2)
  for w in weights_list:
     S = ca.dot(w)
     # low indices: can_inc and lowest S
     if can_inc.any():
       low_all=np.where(can_inc)[0]
       S_low=S[low_all]
       k_low=min(K, len(low_all))
       low=low_all[np.argsort(S_low)[:k_low]]
     else: low=[]
     if can_dec.any():
       high_all=np.where(can_dec)[0]
       S_high=S[high_all]
       k_high=min(K, len(high_all))
       high=high_all[np.argsort(-S_high)[:k_high]]
     else: high=[]
     for i in low:
        for j in high:
           add_pair
  # per active lag
  for a in range(min(A, 20)):
     col=ca[:,a]
     low_pos=np.where(can_inc)[0]; high_pos=np.where(can_dec)[0]
     if len(low_pos)>0:
        low_arr=low_pos[np.argsort(col[low_pos])[:Kper]]
     else: low_arr=[]
     if len(high_pos)>0:
        high_arr=high_pos[np.argsort(-col[high_pos])[:Kper]]
     else: high_arr=[]
     for i in low_arr:
       for j in high_arr: add
  # If still few, add some random / all extremes? Use deterministic based on h? Could add top S low/high last.
  return pairs
```

`make_deltas` details:
```
def make_deltas(c,d,plus,minus,cmax,M,n):
   seq=[]
   # tau pos
   if plus>1e-10:
      pos=d > 1e-12
      if np.any(pos):
         tau = np.min((cmax - c[pos]) / d[pos])
         if tau < 0: tau=0
         maxpos = min(plus, tau)
      else: maxpos=plus
      if maxpos > 1e-10:
         for f in (0.8,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.05,0.02): seq.append(f*maxpos)
      # fixed small independent
      small=min(plus, max(1e-4, 0.1*min(plus,0.2)?)) maybe just plus fractions already. If tau=0, maxpos=0, no pos. Add a few fixed for nonmonotone:
      for f in (0.1,0.2,0.5): if f*plus < 0.35: seq.append(f*plus)
   if minus>1e-10:
      neg=d < -1e-12
      if np.any(neg):
         tau=np.min((cmax-c[neg])/(-d[neg]))
         if tau<0: tau=0
         maxneg=min(minus,tau)
      else: maxneg=minus
      if maxneg>1e-10: for f ... seq.append(-f*maxneg)
      for f: seq.append(-f*minus) if ...
   # unique
   uniq=[]
   for delta in seq:
      if abs(delta)<1e-13: continue
      if delta > 0 and delta > plus*(1-1e-12): continue
      if delta < 0 and -delta > minus*(1-1e-12): continue
      if not any(abs(delta-u)<1e-8 for u in uniq): uniq.append(delta)
   return uniq
```

Better: fixed grid: `if plus > 1e-4: for f in (0.05,0.1,0.2): delta=f*plus`. If plus big, 0.2 maybe too big but eval only; could be okay. To limit, use `small_plus = min(plus, max(1e-4, 0.05))`; add fractions [0.5,1.0] of small_plus. Similarly neg. This catches k=0 concave reduction needing δ~0.05. We'll do:

```

Trial max function with allocation:
```
def trial_max_pair(c,d,delta,n,i,j):
    arr = c + delta*d
    qidx = n-1
    arr[qidx] += -2.0*delta*delta
    dabs = abs(i-j)
    if dabs:
       arr[qidx + dabs] += delta*delta
       arr[qidx - dabs] += delta*delta
    return float(arr.max())
```
Since arr is new each call, modifying okay. If dabs=0 (i==j skip). If dabs>n-1? no. qidx±dabs within 0..2n-2.

Update c with best delta using same arr? We can recompute arr for best (or store `best_arr` in evaluate? To avoid recompute, in evaluate return best_delta and maybe best_arr? But arr is local. We can recompute after selecting (cost small). Or in loop when new best, store `best_arr = arr.copy()`? Memory. Just recompute.

Update:
```
c += best_delta * d_best
c[n-1] += -2*best_delta**2

h[i]+=best_delta; h[j]-=best_delta; clip
```

`active_lp_step`:
```
def active_lp_step(h,n,c,act,rho):
   A=len(act)
   nvar=n+1
   c_obj=np.zeros(nvar); c_obj[-1]=1.0
   ca=active_coef_matrix(h,n,act)
   A_ub=np.zeros((A,nvar)); b_ub=-c[act]
   A_ub[:, :n]=ca.T  # shape A,n
   A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
   lower=np.maximum(-rho, -h)
   upper=np.minimum(rho, 1.0-h)
   bounds=list(zip(lower, upper)) + [(None, None)]
   A_eq=np.zeros((1,nvar)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0; b_eq=[0.0]
   try: res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds, method='highs')
   except: return None
   if not res.success: return None
   z=res.x[:n]
   if np.max(np.abs(z))<1e-12: return None
   return z
```
But hiGHS with t unbounded may be slow; set t bounds `(0.0, c.max()+1e6)` perhaps. Since t should be near cmax. Set `t_lo = 0.0`, `t_hi = 10*(c.max()+1.0)`. If no feasible due t_hi too small, increase. Use `t_hi=100*(c.max()+1)`. If cmax up to 500, 50k. Good.

```
def line_search_vector(h,n,c,z,theta_max, n_eval=12):
   best_theta=0; best_count=c.max()
   if theta_max <=0: return None
   # generate theta fractions of min(theta_max, 5? maybe)
   scale=min(theta_max, 5.0) if theta_max>1 else theta_max
   for f in np.linspace(0, scale, n_eval)[1:]:
      h_t = h + f*z
      if np.any(h_t< -1e-9) or h_t>1+1e-9: continue
      c_t=compute_c_counts(h_t)
      mt=c_t.max()
      if mt < best_count - 1e-12: update
   if best_theta>0: return best_theta, h+best_theta*z, best_count
```

Let's write helpers carefully:

```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if len(h) != n: h=np.resize? should not.
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13 * max(1.0,n): break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask): break
            room = 1.0 - h[mask]
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h[mask] += diff * room / tot
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask): break
            tot = h[mask].sum()
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h[mask] += diff * h[mask] / tot
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
This scaling reduces proportionally to current h; if many zeros, fine. It may leave sum slightly off due underflow; okay.
Could do a simpler two-pass: if diff>0, add to h[mask] equal diff/count; if some exceed 1, redistribute. The room-proportional method avoids exceed.

`interpolate_h`:
```
def interpolate_h(h, n_new):
    n_old=len(h)
    if n_new == n_old: return h.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return h_new
```

`random_lowpass_h`:
Use `np.random.RandomState(seed)`.
If n not even, still target float; threshold count int(n/2). We'll choose m even.

```
def random_lowpass_h(n, seed, window_frac=0.08, sine_mode=True):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    z=rng.randn(n)
    k=max(3, int(n*window_frac))
    if k%2==0: k+=1
    kernel=np.ones(k)/k
    smooth=np.convolve(z, kernel, mode='same')
    if sine_mode:
        x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
        f=int(rng.randint(1,5))
        phase=rng.uniform(0.0, 2*np.pi)
        amp=0.75*(smooth.std()+1e-12)*rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)
        smooth = smooth + amp*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*f*x+phase)
    target=int(round(0.5*n))
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    # If target <=0 or >=n adjust
    order=np.argsort(-smooth)
    h[order[:target]]=1.0
    # Add a little random fractional? no
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
Could also generate smooth continuous not binary: h=clip(0.5+0.5*smooth/ (3std))? But binary easier. Maybe mixture: after threshold, add small noise and project to create fractional? Coarse pair_descent will fractionalize.

`smooth_downup(h,m)`:
```
def downup_variant(h,m):
   h_d=np.array(interpolate_h(h,m))
   # optional smooth by moving average
   k=3; if m>=3: h_d=np.convolve(h_d, np.ones(k)/k, mode='same')
   return interpolate_h(h_d,len(h))
```
Use as alt start.

Pair_descent with LP fallback:
```
def pair_descent(h, n, deadline, active_cap=100, candidate_cap=350, max_no_improve=100, min_tol=0.25, tol_factor=0.01, recompute_every=40, lp_rho=0.02, max_lp_calls=10):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c_counts(h)
    best_c=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy()
    no_improve=0; it=0; lp_calls=0; last_recompute=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if it - last_recompute >= recompute_every:
          h=project_feasible(h,n)
          c=compute_c_counts(h)
          last_recompute=it
          if c.max() < best_c: best_c=c.max(); best_h=h.copy()
       cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax >= best_c - 1e-13 and h is not best? hmm.
       # active
       tol=max(min_tol, tol_factor*max(1.0,best_c))
       idx_all=np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
       if idx_all.size > active_cap:
          idx=idx_all[np.argsort(-c[idx_all])[:active_cap]]
       else: idx=idx_all
       if idx.size < 10:
          idx=np.argsort(-c)[:min(active_cap,c.size)]
       ca=active_coef_matrix(h,n,idx)
       pairs=generate_candidate_pairs(h,n,ca,idx,c,cmax,candidate_cap)
       improved=False
       if pairs:
          coords=[]
          for i,j in pairs: coords.extend((i,j))
          # limit unique coords
          uniq=np.unique(np.asarray(coords))
          if uniq.size > 800: # reduce pairs? take first candidate_cap maybe
              # create new pairs using first
              pairs = pairs[:200]
              coords=[]...
          coef_dict=coefficient_vectors_for_indices(h,n,uniq)
          best_delta=0.0; best_count=cmax; best_pair=None; best_d=None
          for i,j in pairs:
             ci=coef_dict[int(i)]; cj=coef_dict[int(j)]
             d=ci-cj
             plus = min(float(1.0-h[i]), float(h[j]))
             minus = min(float(h[i]), float(1.0-h[j]))
             if plus < 1e-12 and minus < 1e-12: continue
             deltas=make_deltas(c,d,plus,minus,cmax)
             for delta in deltas:
                 m = trial_max_pair(c,d,delta,n,i,j)
                 if m < best_count - 1e-12:
                    best_count=m; best_delta=delta; best_pair=(i,j); best_d=d
          if best_pair is not None:
             i,j=best_pair; delta=best_delta; d=best_d
             # if best_count < best_c - tol_improve
             if best_count < best_c - 1e-10:
                c += delta*d
                qidx=n-1; dd=delta*delta
                c[qidx] += -2.0*dd
                dabs=abs(i-j)
                if dabs:
                   c[qidx+dabs] += dd
                   c[qidx-dabs] += dd
                h[i]+=delta; h[j]-=delta
                h[i]=min(1.0,max(0.0,h[i])); h[j]=min(1.0,max(0.0,h[j]))
                best_c=best_count; best_h=h.copy()
                no_improve=0
                improved=True
             else:
                no_improve += 1
       if not improved:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > 5 and lp_calls < max_lp_calls and time.time() < deadline - 2:
             lp_calls += 1
             z=active_lp_step(h,n,c,idx,lp_rho)
             if z is not None:
                # line search vector
                res=line_search_vector(h,n,c,z,theta_max=3.0,n_eval=12)
                if res is not None:
                   theta,h_new,c_new,count=res
                   if count < best_c - 1e-10:
                      h=h_new; c=c_new; best_c=count; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0; improved=True
       if not improved:
          if no_improve > max_no_improve: break
       it +=1
       if it % 20 ==0 and time.time() > deadline: break
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c_counts(h); best_c2=float(c.max())
    if best_c2 < best_c:
       return h, best_c2
    else:
       h=project_feasible(best_h,n); c=compute_c_counts(h); return h, float(c.max())
```

LP line search: If z from LP has max norm rho=0.02. theta_max compute from bounds:
```
def line_search_vector(h,n,c,z,theta_max,max_scale=3.0):
    pos = z > 0
    neg = z < 0
    if pos.any(): theta_pos = np.min((1-h[pos])/z[pos])
    if neg.any(): theta_neg = np.min(h[neg]/(-z[neg]))
    th = min(theta_pos if pos.any() else inf, theta_neg if neg.any() else inf, max_scale)
    best_count=c.max(); best_theta=0; best_h=h; best_c=c
    for th_val in (th*f for f in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0]):
       if th_val <= 1e-10: continue
       ht = h + th_val*z
       # due round clip? if outside by 1e-10 skip/clip
       if ht.min() < -1e-9 or ht.max() > 1+1e-9: continue
       ct=compute_c_counts(ht)
       mt=float(ct.max())
       if mt < best_count - 1e-9:
          best_count=mt; best_theta=th_val; best_h=ht; best_c=ct
    if best_theta == 0: return None
    return best_theta, project_feasible(best_h,n), compute_c_counts(best_h), best_count
```
But returned c before projection may not match projected; return projected c final. If projection worsens, maybe not accept? We can compare projected count and unprojected; if projected > unprojected + 1e-7, maybe still accept if projected < best. Use final projected c.

```

```

`perturb_and_mix`:
```
def perturb_h(h,n,seed,strength=0.03):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  h2=h.copy()
  m=max(1,n//10)
  for _ in range(m):
    i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
    if i!=j:
       step=strength*rng.uniform(0.25,1.0)
       if rng.rand()<0.5:
          if h2[i]<=1-step and h2[j]>=step: h2[i]+=step; h2[j]-=step
       else:
          if h2[j]<=1-step and h2[i]>=step: h2[j]+=step; h2[i]-=step
  h2=project_feasible(h2,n)
  return h2
```

```

```
def line_search_vector(h,n,c,z,theta_max=3.0, n_eval=10):
   target=0.5*n
   pos = z>0; neg=z<0
   th = theta_max
   if pos.any(): th = min(th, np.min((1-h[pos])/z[pos])*(1-1e-12))
   if neg.any(): th = min(th, np.min(h[neg]/(-z[neg]))*(1-1e-12))
   if th <= 1e-10: return None
   best_count=float(c.max()); best_theta=0.0; best_hp=None; best_cp=None
   fracs=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.45,0.6,0.75,0.9,1.0]
   for f in fracs:
      tv=f*th
      if tv<=1e-10: continue
      hh=h+tv*z
      hh=np.clip(hh,0,1)
      if abs(hh.sum()-target)>1e-10: hh=project_feasible(hh,n)
      ct=compute_c_counts(hh)
      mt=float(ct.max())
      if mt < best_count - 1e-9:
          best_count=mt; best_theta=tv; best_hp=hh; best_cp=ct
   if best_hp is None: return None
   return best_theta, best_hp, best_cp, best_count

Now, could our exact pair update formula with q be incorrect for np c due cross term sign for q? Let's re-derive for np c_r = ∑ h_j (1 - h_{j-r}). Suppose pair i inc, j dec. For a cross term where both coordinates in same product:
Case 1: j = i + r? Then term h_j(1-h_i) at r = j-i? Product new (h_j-δ)(1-(h_i+δ)) = (a-δ)(b-δ) = ab -δa -δb +δ². q +1.
Case 2: i = j + r? term h_i(1-h_j) at r=i-j. New (h_i+δ)(1-(h_j-δ)) = (a+δ)(b+δ)=ab+δa+δb+δ². q +1.
For r=0 self: term h_i(1-h_i): change for i: δ(1-2h_i)-δ², for j: -δ(1-2h_j)-δ², total q -2. Good. So q +1 at lags r = i-j and r=j-i, which are ±|i-j|. Since c array index = n-1+r. Add δ² to both. Good.

Now, `trial_max_pair` assumes q at both ±d. What if d = 0? skip pair. If d = n-1, indices n-1±(n-1) -> 0 and 2n-2 valid. Good.

Does q depend on whether both coordinates appear in valid overlap? For a specific lag r, the product term exists only if both j and j-r valid. For pair positions, r = i-j: term h_i(1-h_j) valid if i in [0,n-1] and i-r=j in [0,n-1] -> yes. r=j-i: term h_j(1-h_i) valid if j in [0,n-1], j-r=i -> yes. So always valid. Good.

```
def coefficient_vectors_for_indices(h,n,indices):
    res={}
    for idx in indices:
       i=int(idx)
       ccoef=np.zeros(2*n-1,float)
       ccoef[n-1+i : i-1 : -1] += 1.0 - h
       ccoef[n-1-i : 2*n-1-i] += -h
       res[i]=ccoef
    return res
```
This recomputes each. If `indices` is numpy array of int. Good.

def objective_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return 1.0
    return float(np.max(compute_c_counts(h)) * (2.0 / float(n)))
```

def multistart_coarse(best_h, best_n, best_c5, deadline, seed_base):
    c5=best_c5
    # m list ensure even, less than best_n maybe
    ms=[]
    for m in (48, 80, 128, 160):
       if m < 16: continue
       if m < best_n: ms.append(m)
    ms=sorted(set(ms))[:3]
    for m in ms:
       for trial in range(4):
          if time.time() > deadline: return best_h,c5
          per_time = min(10.0, max(3.0, (deadline-time.time())/ (4*len(ms))))
          if trial ==0:
             h_m=interpolate_h(best_h,m) if best_n>=m else np.resize(best_h,m)?
          else: h_m=random_lowpass_h(m, seed_base+trial, 0.05+0.02*(trial%3))
          h_m=project_feasible(h_m,m)
          h_m,cnt_m=pair_descent(h_m,m,min(deadline,time.time()+per_time), active_cap=min(32,m), candidate_cap=120, max_no_improve=50, min_tol=0.2, tol_factor=0.03, recompute_every=20, max_lp_calls=2)
          h_b=interpolate_h(h_m,best_n); h_b=project_feasible(h_b,best_n)
          c=objective_c5(h_b)
          if c < c5 - 1e-12:
             best_h=h_b; c5=c
          # if close, quick base polish?
    return best_h,c5
```

Detailed pair_descent code:
```
def pair_descent(h, n, deadline, active_cap=100, candidate_cap=280, max_no_improve=120, min_tol=0.25, tol_factor=0.008, recompute_every=40, max_lp_calls=10, lp_base_rho=0.02):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c_counts(h)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_rec = 0
    lp_calls = 0
    improve_tol = 1e-8
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if it - last_rec >= recompute_every:
          h = project_feasible(h, n)
          c = compute_c_counts(h)
          cur = float(c.max())
          if cur < best_c:
             best_c = cur; best_h = h.copy()
          elif cur > best_c + 1e-6:
             h = best_h.copy(); c = compute_c_counts(h)
          last_rec = it
       cmax = float(c.max())
       # if cmax already below target? not know.
       tol = max(min_tol, tol_factor * max(1.0, best_c))
       idx_all = np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
       if idx_all.size > active_cap:
          idx = idx_all[np.argsort(-c[idx_all])[:active_cap]]
       else:
          idx = idx_all
       if idx.size < 8:
          idx = np.argsort(-c)[:min(active_cap, c.size)]
       ca = active_coef_matrix(h, n, idx)
       improved = False
       pairs = generate_candidate_pairs(h, n, ca, idx, c, cmax, candidate_cap)
       if pairs:
          coords = np.empty(0, dtype=int)
          for i,j in pairs: coords = np.concatenate((coords, np.array([i,j], dtype=int)))
          uniq = np.unique(coords)
          if uniq.size > 900:
             pairs = pairs[:200]
             coords=[]; for ...
             uniq=np.unique(...)
          coef_dict = coefficient_vectors_for_indices(h, n, uniq)
          best_delta=0.0; best_count=cmax; best_pair=None; best_d=None
          for i,j in pairs:
             i=int(i); j=int(j)
             if i==j: continue
             ci=coef_dict[i]; cj=coef_dict[j]
             d=ci-cj
             plus = min(float(1.0 - h[i]), float(h[j]))
             minus = min(float(h[i]), float(1.0 - h[j]))
             if plus < 1e-12 and minus < 1e-12: continue
             deltas = make_deltas(c, d, plus, minus, cmax)
             for delta in deltas:
                 m = trial_max_pair(c, d, delta, n, i, j)
                 if m < best_count - improve_tol:
                     best_count = m; best_delta = delta; best_pair=(i,j); best_d=d
          if best_pair is not None:
             i,j = best_pair; delta = best_delta; d = best_d
             c += delta * d
             qidx = n - 1
             dd = delta * delta
             c[qidx] += -2.0 * dd
             dabs = abs(i-j)
             if dabs:
                c[qidx + dabs] += dd
                c[qidx - dabs] += dd
             h[i] += delta; h[j] -= delta
             if h[i] < 0.0 or h[i] > 1.0 or h[j] < 0.0 or h[j] > 1.0:
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
             if best_count < best_c - improve_tol:
                best_c = best_count; best_h = h.copy()
                no_improve = 0; improved = True
             else:
                no_improve += 1
       if not improved:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve >= 10 and lp_calls < max_lp_calls and time.time() < deadline - 1:
             z = None
             rho = lp_base_rho + 0.015 * (lp_calls % 3)
             z = active_lp_step(h, n, c, idx, rho)
             lp_calls += 1
             if z is not None:
                res = line_search_vector(h, n, c, z, theta_max=3.0)
                if res is not None:
                   theta, h_new, c_new, cnt = res
                   if cnt < best_c - improve_tol:
                      h = h_new; c = c_new; best_c = cnt; best_h = h.copy(); no_improve=0; improved=True
       if not improved:
          if no_improve > max_no_improve:
             break
       it += 1
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c_counts(h)
    cur = float(c.max())
    h2 = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c2 = compute_c_counts(h2)
    cur2 = float(c2.max())
    if cur2 <= cur:
       return h2, cur2
    return h, cur
```
Need be careful: If LP improved, `improved=True`, no break. If pair improved but not best? no.

In pair_descent, if `h` was restored to best_h after recompute worsen, current `c` recompute, but `no_improve` continues. Good.

`line_search_vector` returns projected h/c. It modifies h? no.

`active_lp_step`: If `active_coef_matrix` for idx maybe A=0. If n small, use.

`make_deltas` unique uses `for u in uniq` each time; small. Ensure if `maxpos` is nan: if pos.any but d positive and diff/d inf? cmax-c could be 0/d; okay. If cmax-c negative small, tau negative clipped. Use `tau = float(np.min(...))`; if not finite, tau=0.

Add small fixed deltas even if `maxpos` zero? We append after regardless plus:
```
if plus > 1e-10:
   small = min(plus, 0.05)
   for f in (0.25,0.5,1.0): seq.append(f*small)
```
This can include maxstep duplicates. Good.

`trial_max_pair`: `arr = c + delta * d`; if delta=0 skip. Then `arr[qidx] += -2*dd`, etc. `qidx ± dabs` valid if dabs <= n-1; `abs(i-j)` <= n-1. If dabs=0 skip. Return `float(np.max(arr))`.

One possible performance improvement: Precompute q additions inside loop manually? Fine.

Now, let's think about objective if h has values slightly outside due LP? We project in line_search. Pair h clip may change c not incrementally if clipped significantly. But δ within bounds so no. If h values from previous had tiny outside, plus/minus may be wrong; recompute/project every 40 moves.

If parent n not 500 but say 1000, coarse m<1000 and interpolate to base_n. Main pair_descent at n=1000. High reserve smaller because best_n >=1000. Need set high_reserve accordingly.

`high_n` selection:
```
if best_n < 800: high_n = max(800, min(1000, 2*best_n))
elif best_n < 1200: high_n = 1200
else: high_n = best_n
high_n = 2*(high_n//2)
if high_n > best_n and time...
```
If base_n=500, high=1000. If base_n=800, high=1200 maybe. Pair_descent at 1200 active cap 100 candidate 250, okay.

`main_base_deadline`: high_reserve = 180.0 if best_n < 1000 else 50.0. If best_n=1000, reserve 50. If best_n=1200, 50. We can still attempt higher 1200? skip if no time. Good.

Perturb attempts in main: `attempt < 12` but time controlled. Use `chunk_time = 150.0` maybe. If pair_descent runs to chunk_deadline and improves, we continue same best_h (not perturbed). If no improve, next odd attempt perturb.
```
start_h = best_h.copy()
if attempt > 0 and (attempt % 2 == 1):
   start_h = perturb_h(best_h, best_n, seed + 3000 + attempt, strength=0.02 + 0.01*(attempt%3))
   if attempt % 4 == 3:
      rnd = random_lowpass_h(best_n, seed + 5000 + attempt, 0.06)
      start_h = project_feasible(0.7*best_h + 0.3*rnd, best_n)
h_curr, cnt = pair_descent(start_h, best_n, chunk_deadline, ...)
c5=cnt*2/best_n
if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-12: update
```
`pair_descent` if start_h is perturbed worse may return start_h unimproved with c5 worse; ignore.

Potential: Pair_descent from start_h tracks its own best, so if start_h worse but finds a local optimum below global, returns that h/count? It returns best from start path. If best from start path > start? It returns count lower than start but maybe still >global. We compare. Good.

If pair_descent from perturb explores a basin but returns a count not lower than global but the trajectory might have a point lower? It tracks best, so if any lower it returns. Good.

Finally, after base main, update `best_c5 = objective_c5(best_h)` to be safe.

Time: `pair_descent` may use `time.time()` many times; fine.

Potential issue: `np.argsort` with `np.inf`? col finite. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if len(h) != n:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h))
        h = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n), x_old, h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(300):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13 * max(1.0, n):
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            room = 1.0 - h[mask]
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h[mask] += diff * room / tot
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            tot = h[mask].sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h[mask] += diff * h[mask] / tot
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def compute_c_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").astype(float, copy=False)


def objective_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return 1.0
    return float(np.max(compute_c_counts(h)) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def interpolate_h(h, n_new):
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_new == n_old:
        return h.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)


def coefficient_vectors_for_indices(h, n, indices):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    out = {}
    base = n - 1
    for idx in indices:
        i = int(idx)
        vec = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        vec[base + i : i - 1 : -1] += 1.0 - h
        vec[base - i : 2 * n - 1 - i] += -h
        out[i] = vec
    return out


def active_coef_matrix(h, n, act):
    a = act.size
    ca = np.zeros((n, a), dtype=float)
    if a == 0:
        return ca
    base = n - 1
    for t, mm in enumerate(act):
        r = int(mm) - base
        if r >= 0:
            if r == 0:
                ca[:, t] += 1.0 - h
                ca[:, t] -= h
            else:
                k = n - r
                ca[r:n, t] += 1.0 - h[:k]
                ca[:k, t] += -h[r:]
        else:
            l = -r
            k = n - l
            ca[:k, t] += 1.0 - h[l:]
            ca[l:, t] += -h[:k]
    return ca


def make_deltas(c, d, plus, minus, cmax):
    seq = []
    if plus > 1e-11:
        pos = d > 1e-12
        if np.any(pos):
            diff = cmax - c[pos]
            diff = np.maximum(diff, 0.0)
            tau = float(np.min(diff / d[pos]))
            if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau < 0.0:
                tau = 0.0
            maxpos = min(plus, tau)
        else:
            maxpos = plus
        if maxpos > 1e-10:
            for f in (0.8, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15, 0.05, 0.02):
                seq.append(f * maxpos)
        small = min(plus, 0.05)
        if small > 1e-10:
            for f in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
                seq.append(f * small)
    if minus > 1e-11:
        neg = d < -1e-12
        if np.any(neg):
            diff = cmax - c[neg]
            diff = np.maximum(diff, 0.0)
            tau = float(np.min(diff / (-d[neg])))
            if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau < 0.0:
                tau = 0.0
            maxneg = min(minus, tau)
        else:
            maxneg = minus
        if maxneg > 1e-10:
            for f in (0.8, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15, 0.05, 0.02):
                seq.append(-f * maxneg)
        small = min(minus, 0.05)
        if small > 1e-10:
            for f in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
                seq.append(-f * small)

    uniq = []
    for delta in seq:
        if abs(delta) < 1e-13:
            continue
        if delta > 0.0 and delta > plus * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            continue
        if delta < 0.0 and -delta > minus * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            continue
        ok = True
        for u in uniq:
            if abs(u - delta) < 1e-9:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            uniq.append(delta)
    return uniq


def trial_max_pair(c, d, delta, n, i, j):
    arr = c + delta * d
    q = n - 1
    dd = delta * delta
    arr[q] += -2.0 * dd
    dabs = abs(i - j)
    if dabs:
        arr[q + dabs] += dd
        arr[q - dabs] += dd
    return float(np.max(arr))


def _add_pair_to_list(pairs, seen, n, i, j, cap):
    i = int(i)
    j = int(j)
    if i == j:
        return
    if i > j:
        i, j = j, i
    key = i * n + j
    if key not in seen and len(pairs) < cap:
        seen.add(key)
        pairs.append((i, j))


def generate_candidate_pairs(h, n, ca, idx, c, cmax, cap):
    pairs = []
    seen = set()
    a = idx.size
    if a == 0:
        return pairs

    low_pool = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)[0]
    high_pool = np.where(h > 1.0 + 1e-12)[0]
    if low_pool.size == 0 or high_pool.size == 0:
        low_pool = np.arange(n)
        high_pool = np.arange(n)

    s = ca.dot(None)  # placeholder to avoid unused warning
    weights = []

    w0 = np.ones(a, dtype=float)
    weights.append(w0 / w0.sum())

    topc = c[idx]
    x = topc - topc.max()
    w1 = np.exp(5.0 * x)
    sw = w1.sum()
    if sw > 0.0:
        weights.append(w1 / sw)

    if a > 10:
        order = np.argsort(-topc)[:min(20, a)]
        w2 = np.zeros(a, dtype=float)
        vals = topc[order] - topc[order].max()
        w2[order] = np.exp(40.0 * vals)
        sw2 = w2.sum()
        if sw2 > 0.0:
            weights.append(w2 / sw2)

    k_w = 10
    for w in weights:
        score = ca.dot(w)
        sl = score[low_pool]
        ol = np.argsort(sl)[:min(k_w, low_pool.size)]
        low = low_pool[ol]
        sh = score[high_pool]
        oh = np.argsort(-sh)[:min(k_w, high_pool.size)]
        high = high_pool[oh]
        for i in low:
            for j in high:
                _add_pair_to_list(pairs, seen, n, i, j, cap)
                if len(pairs) >= cap:
                    return pairs

    k_p = 3
    for a_idx in range(min(a, 16)):
        col = ca[:, a_idx]
        cl = col[low_pool]
        ol = np.argsort(cl)[:min(k_p, low_pool.size)]
        low = low_pool[ol]
        ch = col[high_pool]
        oh = np.argsort(-ch)[:min(k_p, high_pool.size)]
        high = high_pool[oh]
        for i in low:
            for j in high:
                _add_pair_to_list(pairs, seen, n, i, j, cap)
                if len(pairs) >= cap:
                    return pairs

    if not pairs:
        if low_pool.size and high_pool.size:
            i = int(low_pool[0])
            j = int(high_pool[0])
            _add_pair_to_list(pairs, seen, n, i, j, cap)
    return pairs


def active_lp_step(h, n, c, act, rho):
    a = act.size
    if a == 0:
        return None
    ca = active_coef_matrix(h, n, act)
    nvar = n + 1
    c_obj = np.zeros(nvar, dtype=float)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0

    A_ub = np.zeros((a, nvar), dtype=float)
    A_ub[:, :n] = ca.T
    A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
    b_ub = -c[act].astype(float, copy=True)

    lower = np.maximum(-rho, -h)
    upper = np.minimum(rho, 1.0 - h)
    bounds = []
    for i in range(n):
        lo = float(lower[i])
        hi = float(upper[i])
        if lo > hi:
            val = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            lo = hi = val
        bounds.append((lo, hi))
    cmax = float(c.max())
    bounds.append((0.0, 100.0 * (cmax + 1.0) + 10.0))

    A_eq = np.zeros((1, nvar), dtype=float)
    A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.zeros(1, dtype=float)

    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                      bounds=bounds, method="highs")
        if not res.success:
            return None
        z = res.x[:n].astype(float, copy=True)
        if np.max(np.abs(z)) < 1e-13:
            return None
        z -= z.mean()
        return z
    except Exception:
        return None


def line_search_vector(h, n, c, z, theta_max=3.0):
    pos = z > 0.0
    neg = z < 0.0
    th = float(theta_max)
    if np.any(pos):
        th = min(th, float(np.min((1.0 - h[pos]) / z[pos])) * (1.0 - 1e-12))
    if np.any(neg):
        th = min(th, float(np.min(h[neg] / (-z[neg]))) * (1.0 - 1e-12))
    if th <= 1e-10:
        return None

    start_c = float(c.max())
    best_theta = 0.0
    best_count = start_c
    best_h = None
    best_cvec = None
    target = 0.5 * n

    fracs = (0.05, 0.1, 0.18, 0.3, 0.45, 0.6, 0.75, 0.9, 1.0)
    for f in fracs:
        tv = f * th
        if tv <= 1e-12:
            continue
        hh = h + tv * z
        hh = np.clip(hh, 0.0, 1.0)
        if abs(hh.sum() - target) > 1e-10:
            hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
        cc = compute_c_counts(hh)
        mt = float(cc.max())
        if mt < best_count - 1e-9:
            best_count = mt
            best_theta = tv
            best_h = hh
            best_cvec = cc
    if best_h is None:
        return None
    return best_theta, best_h, best_cvec, best_count


def pair_descent(h, n, deadline,
                 active_cap=100,
                 candidate_cap=260,
                 max_no_improve=120,
                 min_tol=0.25,
                 tol_factor=0.008,
                 recompute_every=40,
                 max_lp_calls=10,
                 lp_base_rho=0.02):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c_counts(h)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_rec = 0
    lp_calls = 0
    improve_tol = 1e-8

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if it - last_rec >= recompute_every:
            h2 = project_feasible(h, n)
            c2 = compute_c_counts(h2)
            cur2 = float(c2.max())
            if cur2 < best_c:
                best_c = cur2
                best_h = h2.copy()
                h = h2
                c = c2
            elif cur2 > best_c + 1e-6:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = compute_c_counts(h)
            last_rec = it

        cmax = float(c.max())
        tol = max(min_tol, tol_factor * max(1.0, best_c))
        idx_all = np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
        if idx_all.size > active_cap:
            idx = idx_all[np.argsort(-c[idx_all])[:active_cap]]
        else:
            idx = idx_all
        if idx.size < 8:
            idx = np.argsort(-c)[:min(active_cap, c.size)]
        ca = active_coef_matrix(h, n, idx)

        improved = False
        pairs = generate_candidate_pairs(h, n, ca, idx, c, cmax, candidate_cap)
        if pairs:
            coords = []
            for i, j in pairs:
                coords.append(int(i))
                coords.append(int(j))
            uniq = np.unique(np.asarray(coords, dtype=int))
            if uniq.size > 900:
                pairs = pairs[:200]
                coords = []
                for i, j in pairs:
                    coords.append(int(i))
                    coords.append(int(j))
                uniq = np.unique(np.asarray(coords, dtype=int))

            coef_dict = coefficient_vectors_for_indices(h, n, uniq)
            best_delta = 0.0
            best_pair = None
            best_d = None
            best_new = cmax

            for i, j in pairs:
                i = int(i)
                j = int(j)
                if i == j:
                    continue
                ci = coef_dict[i]
                cj = coef_dict[j]
                d = ci - cj
                plus = min(float(1.0 - h[i]), float(h[j]))
                minus = min(float(h[i]), float(1.0 - h[j]))
                if plus < 1e-12 and minus < 1e-12:
                    continue
                deltas = make_deltas(c, d, plus, minus, cmax)
                for delta in deltas:
                    val = trial_max_pair(c, d, delta, n, i, j)
                    if val < best_new - improve_tol:
                        best_new = val
                        best_delta = delta
                        best_pair = (i, j)
                        best_d = d

            if best_pair is not None:
                i, j = best_pair
                delta = best_delta
                d = best_d
                c += delta * d
                q = n - 1
                dd = delta * delta
                c[q] += -2.0 * dd
                dabs = abs(i - j)
                if dabs:
                    c[q + dabs] += dd
                    c[q - dabs] += dd

                h[i] += delta
                h[j] -= delta
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

                if best_new < best_c - improve_tol:
                    best_c = best_new
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    improved = True
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 10 and lp_calls < max_lp_calls and time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
                rho = lp_base_rho + 0.015 * (lp_calls % 3)
                z = active_lp_step(h, n, c, idx, rho)
                lp_calls += 1
                if z is not None:
                    res = line_search_vector(h, n, c, z, theta_max=3.0)
                    if res is not None:
                        theta, h_new, c_new, cnt = res
                        if cnt < best_c - improve_tol:
                            h = h_new
                            c = c_new
                            best_c = cnt
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            no_improve = 0
                            improved = True

        if not improved and no_improve > max_no_improve:
            break
        it += 1

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c_counts(h)
    cur = float(c.max())
    h2 = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c2 = compute_c_counts(h2)
    cur2 = float(c2.max())
    if cur2 <= cur:
        return h2, cur2
    return h, cur


def random_lowpass_h(n, seed, window_frac=0.08, sine_mode=True):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n = int(n)
    if n < 4:
        return project_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5), n)
    z = rng.randn(n)
    k = max(3, int(n * window_frac))
    if k % 2 == 0:
        k += 1
    kernel = np.ones(k, dtype=float) / k
    smooth = np.convolve(z, kernel, mode="same")
    if sine_mode:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        f = int(rng.randint(1, 5))
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        amp = 0.75 * (float(np.std(smooth)) + 1e-12) * rng.uniform(0.4, 1.4)
        smooth = smooth + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + phase)
    target = int(round(0.5 * n))
    target = max(1, min(n - 1, target))
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(-smooth)
    h[order[:target]] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def perturb_h(h, n, seed, strength=0.03):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h2 = h.copy()
    moves = max(1, n // 10)
    for _ in range(moves):
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        step = strength * rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)
        if rng.rand() < 0.5:
            if h2[i] <= 1.0 - step and h2[j] >= step:
                h2[i] += step
                h2[j] -= step
        else:
            if h2[j] <= 1.0 - step and h2[i] >= step:
                h2[j] += step
                h2[i] -= step
    return project_feasible(h2, n)


def downup_variant(h, m):
    h_old = h
    hd = interpolate_h(h_old, m)
    if m >= 5:
        hd = np.convolve(hd, np.ones(3, dtype=float) / 3.0, mode="same")
    return interpolate_h(hd, len(h_old))


def multistart_coarse(best_h, best_n, best_c5, deadline, seed_base):
    best_c = objective_c5(best_h)
    m_list = []
    for m in (48, 80, 128, 160):
        if 16 <= m < best_n:
            m_list.append(m)
    m_list = sorted(set(m_list))[:3]

    for m in m_list:
        m = 2 * (m // 2)
        if m < 16:
            continue
        for trial in range(4):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
            per_time = min(10.0, max(2.0, rem / max(1, 4 * (len(m_list) - m_list.index(m) + (trial // 4)))))
            if trial == 0 and len(best_h) >= m:
                h_m = interpolate_h(best_h, m)
            elif trial == 1 and len(best_h) >= m:
                h_m = downup_variant(best_h, m)
                h_m = interpolate_h(h_m, m)
            else:
                h_m = random_lowpass_h(m, seed_base + 17 * trial + m,
                                       window_frac=0.05 + 0.02 * (trial % 3))
            h_m = project_feasible(h_m, m)
            t_end = min(deadline, time.time() + per_time)
            if t_end > time.time():
                h_m, _ = pair_descent(
                    h_m, m, t_end,
                    active_cap=min(32, m),
                    candidate_cap=120,
                    max_no_improve=40,
                    min_tol=0.2,
                    tol_factor=0.03,
                    recompute_every=20,
                    max_lp_calls=2,
                )
            hb = interpolate_h(h_m, best_n)
            hb = project_feasible(hb, best_n)
            c5 = objective_c5(hb)
            if c5 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h = hb
                best_c = c5
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(20.0, budget_s - 18.0)
    np.random.seed(int(seed))

    try:
        n_init = int(len(initial_h_values))
        if n_init < 8:
            raise ValueError
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = n_init
    except Exception:
        n = 500
        h = random_lowpass_h(n, int(seed) + 1, 0.08)

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    count0 = compute_c_counts(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_count = float(count0.max())
    best_n = n
    best_c5 = best_count * (2.0 / float(n))

    sym = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    sym = project_feasible(sym, n)
    c5_sym = objective_c5(sym)
    if c5_sym < best_c5 - 1e-12:
        best_h = sym
        best_count = c5_sym * (n / 2.0)
        best_c5 = c5_sym

    for mv in (64, 96, 128):
        mv = 2 * (mv // 2)
        if mv < 16 or mv >= n:
            continue
        hv = downup_variant(best_h, mv)
        hv = project_feasible(hv, n)
        c5_v = objective_c5(hv)
        if c5_v < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h = hv
            best_count = c5_v * (n / 2.0)
            best_c5 = c5_v

    if time.time() < deadline - 600.0:
        coarse_deadline = min(deadline - 420.0, time.time() + 160.0)
        best_h, best_c5 = multistart_coarse(best_h, best_n, best_c5,
                                            coarse_deadline, int(seed) + 373)

    high_reserve = 180.0 if best_n < 1000 else 50.0
    base_deadline = max(time.time() + 1.0, deadline - high_reserve)
    attempt = 0
    while time.time() < base_deadline and attempt < 12:
        chunk = 120.0 if attempt == 0 else 180.0
        chunk_deadline = min(base_deadline, time.time() + chunk)
        start_h = best_h.copy()
        if attempt > 0 and attempt % 2 == 1:
            start_h = perturb_h(best_h, best_n, int(seed) + 4000 + attempt,
                                strength=0.018 + 0.012 * (attempt % 3))
        if attempt > 2 and attempt % 4 == 3:
            rnd = random_lowpass_h(best_n, int(seed) + 6000 + attempt, 0.06)
            start_h = project_feasible(0.72 * best_h + 0.28 * rnd, best_n)

        h_cur, cnt_cur = pair_descent(
            start_h, best_n, chunk_deadline,
            active_cap=min(120, max(30, best_n // 5)),
            candidate_cap=280 if best_n <= 700 else 240,
            max_no_improve=120,
            min_tol=0.25,
            tol_factor=0.008,
            recompute_every=40,
            max_lp_calls=10,
            lp_base_rho=0.02,
        )
        c5_cur = cnt_cur * (2.0 / float(best_n))
        if c5_cur < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h = h_cur
            best_count = cnt_cur
            best_c5 = c5_cur
        attempt += 1

    if time.time() < deadline - 35.0:
        if best_n < 800:
            high_n = max(800, min(1000, 2 * best_n))
        elif best_n < 1200:
            high_n = 1200
        else:
            high_n = best_n
        high_n = 2 * (high_n // 2)
        if high_n > best_n:
            hh = interpolate_h(best_h, high_n)
            hh = project_feasible(hh, high_n)
            c5_hi = objective_c5(hh)
            if c5_hi < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_h = hh
                best_n = high_n
                best_count = c5_hi * (float(high_n) / 2.0)
                best_c5 = c5_hi

            if time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
                hh2, cnt2 = pair_descent(
                    best_h, best_n, deadline - 8.0,
                    active_cap=min(100, max(40, best_n // 8)),
                    candidate_cap=240,
                    max_no_improve=140,
                    min_tol=0.3,
                    tol_factor=0.007,
                    recompute_every=50,
                    max_lp_calls=8,
                    lp_base_rho=0.018,
                )
                c5_hi2 = cnt2 * (2.0 / float(best_n))
                if c5_hi2 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_h = hh2
                    best_count = cnt2
                    best_c5 = c5_hi2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    cvec = compute_c_counts(best_h)
    best_c5 = float(cvec.max() * (2.0 / float(best_n)))
    return best_h, best_c5, int(best_n)
```
